<div class="tab-content active" id="tab1">
Experience cutting-edge performance with this advanced mobile phone featuring 8GB of RAM and 256GB of internal storage. Powered by the Snapdragon 888 processor, it delivers lightning-fast speed for smooth multitasking, gaming, and browsing. The massive storage ensures you can store all your apps, files, and media without worrying about running out of space.
<br><br>
Capture stunning photos and videos with the 50MP triple camera setup, which supports 4K video recording. Whether it's daylight or low-light, the camera's powerful sensors produce sharp, vibrant images. The immersive 4K video recording lets you create cinematic moments on the go, making it ideal for content creators and photography enthusiasts.
<br>

<li>UFS 3.2 technology for faster data transfer.</li></ol>
</div>

<ul class="tabs"><li class="active" data-open="#tab1">Description</li> <li data-open="#tab2">Specification</li></ul> 

<div class="tab-content active" id="tab1">
Brew your perfect cup of coffee effortlessly with this efficient coffee maker, designed to enhance your morning routine. With a powerful brewing system, it ensures a rich, flavorful coffee in just minutes. Whether you prefer a strong espresso or a light brew, this coffee maker can accommodate a wide range of coffee strengths to suit your taste.
<br><br>
The sleek, compact design makes it a perfect fit for any kitchen, without taking up too much counter space. Its user-friendly interface ensures ease of operation with just a few simple steps. The built-in filter guarantees pure, smooth coffee every time, eliminating the need for disposable filters and making it eco-friendly.
  <br>
<h4>Highlights of Our Premium Coffee Maker</h4>
  <ol><li>Powerful brewing system for rich, flavorful coffee</li>
<li>Compact design fits perfectly in any kitchen.</li>
<li>User-friendly controls for easy operation.</li>
<li>Built-in filter for pure, smooth coffee every time.</li>
<li>Automatic shut-off for safety and energy savings.</li></ol>
</div> 

<div class="tab-content" id="tab2">Equipped with an automatic shut-off feature, this coffee maker prioritizes safety and energy efficiency. The detachable water reservoir and drip tray allow for easy cleaning and maintenance, ensuring that your coffee maker stays in top condition for years to come. Start your day right with a reliable, high-quality brew.<h3>Key features</h3> <ol>
<li>Compact design fits perfectly in any kitchen.</li>

<ul class="tabs"><li class="active" data-open="#tab1">Description</li> <li data-open="#tab2">Specification</li></ul> 

<div class="tab-content active" id="tab1">
Stay on top of your fitness goals with this advanced fitness smartwatch, designed to monitor your health and keep you active. Equipped with heart rate monitoring, GPS tracking, and multiple sports modes, this smartwatch provides accurate data for your workouts, helping you optimize your training. Whether you’re running, cycling, or swimming, this watch is your perfect workout companion.
<br><br>
The sleek, stylish design makes it suitable for everyday wear, blending seamlessly with both fitness gear and casual outfits. The high-resolution display offers clear visuals, while the long-lasting battery ensures you stay connected and monitored throughout the day. You can also track your sleep patterns, calorie intake, and steps, ensuring a complete picture of your health and activity.
<br>
<h4>Why Choose Our Advanced Fitness Smartwatch?</h4>
  <ol><li>Heart rate monitor and GPS for accurate tracking</li>
<li>Multiple sports modes for varied workout options.</li>
<li>High-resolution display with long battery life.</li>
<li>Sleep, calorie, and step tracking for health monitoring.</li>
<li>Water-resistant design for all-weather performance.</li></ol>
</div> 

<div class="tab-content" id="tab2">Stay connected on the go with smart notifications for calls, texts, and apps right on your wrist. The watch is also water-resistant, making it suitable for all-weather use and even swimming sessions. With its robust feature set and comfortable design, this fitness smartwatch is the ideal choice for anyone looking to lead a healthier, more active lifestyle.<h3>Key features</h3><ol><li>Multiple sports modes for varied workout options.</li>
<li>High-resolution display with long battery life.</li>
<li>Sleep, calorie, and step tracking for health monitoring.</li>
<li>Water-resistant design for all-weather performance.</li></ol></div><!--[ Product description ends here ]-->

<div class="tab-content active" id="tab1">
Make perfect toast every morning with this high-performance toaster, designed for precision and convenience. Whether you prefer light, golden slices or crispy toast, the adjustable browning settings ensure your toast is exactly how you like it. With wide slots, it accommodates everything from thin slices to thick bagels and artisanal bread.
<br><br>
This toaster features a sleek, modern design that will complement any kitchen décor. The easy-to-use controls make it simple to adjust your settings, while the built-in crumb tray ensures easy cleanup after each use. Its compact size makes it a great fit even for smaller kitchens, offering functionality without taking up too much counter space.
<br>
<h4>Highlights of Our Premium Toaster</h4>
  <ol><li>Adjustable browning for perfect toast every time</li>

<li>Sleek design fits any kitchen décor.</li>
<li>Built-in crumb tray for easy cleaning.</li>
<li>Automatic shut-off for safety and convenience.</li></ol>
</div> 

<div class="tab-content" id="tab2">Safety is a priority with its automatic shut-off feature, which prevents overheating. The defrost, reheat, and cancel functions offer extra versatility, allowing you to toast frozen items or quickly warm up food. This toaster combines style, convenience, and reliable performance to make breakfast preparation quick and easy.<h3>Key features</h3> <ol><li>Wide slots for all types of bread and bagels.</li>

<!--[ Product description starts here ]-->   
<ul class="tabs"><li class="active" data-open="#tab1">Description</li> <li data-open="#tab2">Specification</li></ul> 

<div class="tab-content active" id="tab1">
Enjoy healthier versions of your favorite fried foods with this versatile air fryer. Using advanced air circulation technology, it cooks your meals with little to no oil, ensuring a crispy, golden finish without the extra calories. From fries and chicken wings to veggies and desserts, this air fryer delivers delicious, guilt-free meals in minutes.
<br><br>
The spacious cooking basket allows you to prepare meals for the whole family, while the adjustable temperature settings give you precise control over your cooking. Its rapid heating ensures even cooking, producing perfectly crispy results every time. The non-stick coating makes cleanup a breeze, and the basket is dishwasher-safe for added convenience.<br>
<h4>Why Our Air Fryer is the Perfect Kitchen Companion</h4>
  <ol><li>Advanced air circulation for crispy, oil-free cooking</li>
<li>Spacious basket for family-sized portions.</li>
<li>Adjustable temperature for precise cooking control.</li>
<li>Easy-to-clean, non-stick, and dishwasher-safe basket.</li>
<li>Digital controls for multiple cooking modes.</li></ol>
</div> 

<div class="tab-content" id="tab2">With its sleek, modern design, this air fryer fits perfectly in any kitchen, adding both style and functionality. The easy-to-use digital controls make it simple to select the right cooking mode, whether you're frying, baking, or roasting. Say goodbye to messy, unhealthy oil frying and hello to quick, delicious meals with this innovative air fryer.<h3>Key features</h3> <ol><li>Spacious basket for family-sized portions.</li>
<li>Adjustable temperature for precise cooking control.</li>
<li>Easy-to-clean, non-stick, and dishwasher-safe basket.</li>
<li>Digital controls for multiple cooking modes.</li></ol></div><!--[ Product description ends here ]-->

With a powerful battery, these earbuds offer hours of playback on a single charge, and the included charging case provides additional battery life, keeping your music going all day. Enjoy your favorite tracks, podcasts, or calls without interruptions.

<ul class="tabs"><li class="active" data-open="#tab1">Description</li> <li data-open="#tab2">Specification</li></ul> 

<div class="tab-content active" id="tab1">
Immerse yourself in your music with these premium wireless headphones, designed for audiophiles and casual listeners alike. Featuring cutting-edge audio technology, they deliver rich sound with deep bass and crystal-clear highs, providing an exceptional listening experience for music, podcasts, and movies. Whether you’re at home or on the go, these headphones make every note come alive.
<br><br>
Designed for comfort, these wireless headphones feature plush ear cushions and an adjustable headband, allowing for long listening sessions without discomfort. The lightweight build ensures that you can wear them for hours, making them perfect for travel or extended use during workouts. Plus, with an impressive battery life, you can enjoy your favorite tunes for hours on a single charge.
<br>
<h4>Top Features of Our Wireless Headphones</h4>
  <ol><li>Rich sound with deep bass and clear highs</li>
<li>Comfortable design for long listening sessions.</li>
<li>Impressive battery life for all-day use.</li>
<li>Touch controls for easy music and call management.</li>
<li>Built-in microphone for clear communication.</li></ol>
</div> 

<div class="tab-content" id="tab2">With intuitive touch controls, managing your music and calls is effortless. The built-in microphone ensures clear communication, whether you’re taking calls or engaging with your voice assistant. Experience the freedom of wireless listening with a reliable Bluetooth connection that keeps you connected without the hassle of cords.<h3>Key features</h3> <ol><li>Comfortable design for long listening sessions.</li>
<li>Impressive battery life for all-day use.</li>
<li>Touch controls for easy music and call management.</li>
<li>Built-in microphone for clear communication.</li></ol></div><!--[ Product description ends here ]-->

<div class="tab-content active" id="tab1">
Brew your perfect cup of coffee effortlessly with this efficient coffee maker, designed to enhance your morning routine. With a powerful brewing system, it ensures a rich, flavorful coffee in just minutes. Whether you prefer a strong espresso or a light brew, this coffee maker can accommodate a wide range of coffee strengths to suit your taste.
<br><br>
The sleek, compact design makes it a perfect fit for any kitchen, without taking up too much counter space. Its user-friendly interface ensures ease of operation with just a few simple steps. The built-in filter guarantees pure, smooth coffee every time, eliminating the need for disposable filters and making it eco-friendly.
  <br>
<h4>Highlights of Our Premium Coffee Maker</h4>
  <ol><li>Powerful brewing system for rich, flavorful coffee</li>

<li>Built-in filter for pure, smooth coffee every time.</li>
<li>Automatic shut-off for safety and energy savings.</li></ol>
</div> 

<div class="tab-content" id="tab2">Equipped with an automatic shut-off feature, this coffee maker prioritizes safety and energy efficiency. Start your day right with a reliable, high-quality brew.<h3>Key features</h3> <ol>

<ul class="tabs"><li class="active" data-open="#tab1">Description</li> <li data-open="#tab2">Specification</li></ul> 

<div class="tab-content active" id="tab1">
Make perfect toast every morning with this high-performance toaster, designed for precision and convenience. Whether you prefer light, golden slices or crispy toast, the adjustable browning settings ensure your toast is exactly how you like it. With wide slots, it accommodates everything from thin slices to thick bagels and artisanal bread.
<br><br>
This toaster features a sleek, modern design that will complement any kitchen décor. The easy-to-use controls make it simple to adjust your settings, while the built-in crumb tray ensures easy cleanup after each use. Its compact size makes it a great fit even for smaller kitchens, offering functionality without taking up too much counter space.
<br>
<h4>Highlights of Our Premium Toaster</h4>
  <ol><li>Adjustable browning for perfect toast every time</li>
<li>Wide slots for all types of bread and bagels.</li>
<li>Sleek design fits any kitchen décor.</li>
<li>Built-in crumb tray for easy cleaning.</li>
<li>Automatic shut-off for safety and convenience.</li></ol>
</div> 

<div class="tab-content" id="tab2">Safety is a priority with its automatic shut-off feature, which prevents overheating. The defrost, reheat, and cancel functions offer extra versatility, allowing you to toast frozen items or quickly warm up food. This toaster combines style, convenience, and reliable performance to make breakfast preparation quick and easy.<h3>Key features</h3> <ol><li>Wide slots for all types of bread and bagels.</li>
<li>Sleek design fits any kitchen décor.</li>

<div class="tab-content active" id="tab1">
Enjoy healthier versions of your favorite fried foods with this versatile air fryer. Using advanced air circulation technology, it cooks your meals with little to no oil, ensuring a crispy, golden finish without the extra calories. From fries and chicken wings to veggies and desserts, this air fryer delivers delicious, guilt-free meals in minutes.
<br><br>
The spacious cooking basket allows you to prepare meals for the whole family, while the adjustable temperature settings give you precise control over your cooking. Its rapid heating ensures even cooking, producing perfectly crispy results every time. The non-stick coating makes cleanup a breeze, and the basket is dishwasher-safe for added convenience.<br>
<h4>Why Our Air Fryer is the Perfect Kitchen Companion</h4>
  <ol><li>Advanced air circulation for crispy, oil-free cooking</li>
<li>Spacious basket for family-sized portions.</li>
<li>Adjustable temperature for precise cooking control.</li>
<li>Easy-to-clean, non-stick, and dishwasher-safe basket.</li>
<li>Digital controls for multiple cooking modes.</li></ol>
</div> 

<div class="tab-content" id="tab2">With its sleek, modern design, this air fryer fits perfectly in any kitchen, adding both style and functionality. The easy-to-use digital controls make it simple to select the right cooking mode, whether you're frying, baking, or roasting. Say goodbye to messy, unhealthy oil frying and hello to quick, delicious meals with this innovative air fryer.<h3>Key features</h3> <ol><li>Spacious basket for family-sized portions.</li>

Whether you're going for a subtle glow or a more intense shine, this versatile highlighter adapts to your needs. Its long-lasting wear ensures your glow stays fresh throughout the day, while the lightweight formula feels comfortable on your skin.
